							has_country_flag = caleston_directorate_player
						}
						save_global_event_target_as = caleston_directorate

                    generate_empire_home_planet = yes
				}
				else = {
					generate_caleston_directorate_country = yes
				}
				set_capital = yes
				set_owner = event_target:caleston_directorate

				}
                generate_btr_start_deposits_and_blockers = yes
                btr_generate_canon_planetary_features = yes
                inline_script = "game_start/canon/caleston_dir_origin"

                    create_starting_leaders = yes

			save_global_event_target_as = rachniHomeworld

			}
            prevent_anomaly = yes

            check_for_rachni_player = yes

            if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						has_country_flag = rachni_hive_player
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = rachni_hive_player
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = rachni_hive
				}
                generate_empire_home_planet = yes
            }
			else = {
				generate_rachni_hive_country = yes
			}
            set_capital = yes
			set_owner = event_target:rachni_hive
			create_colony = {
				owner = event_target:rachni_hive
				species = event_target:rachniSpecies
				ethos = owner
			}
            generate_btr_start_deposits_and_blockers = yes
            btr_generate_canon_planetary_features = yes
            inline_script = "game_start/canon/rachni_hiv_origin"
            generate_rac_utukku_pops = yes
            event_target:rachni_hive = {
                create_starting_leaders = yes
                country_event = { id = game_start.9 }
            }
		}
    }
	
	planet = { 
		name = "Inakhos"
		class = pc_asteroid

		orbit_distance = 75

    }

			save_global_event_target_as = gothonesHomeworld
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = event_target:gothonesHomeworld
				}
				log = "Gothones Homeworld Event Target Set"
			}
            prevent_anomaly = yes

            check_for_gothones_player = yes

            if = {
				limit = {
					any_country = {
						has_country_flag = gothones_remnant_player
					}
				}
				random_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = gothones_remnant_player
					}
					save_global_event_target_as = gothones_remnant
				}
                generate_empire_home_planet = yes
			}
			else = {
				generate_gothones_remnant_country = yes
			}
            set_capital = yes
			set_owner = event_target:gothones_remnant
			create_colony = {
				owner = event_target:gothones_remnant
				species = event_target:gothonesSpecies
				ethos = owner
			}
            generate_btr_start_deposits_and_blockers = yes
            btr_generate_canon_planetary_features = yes
            inline_script = "game_start/canon/gothones_rem_origin"
            generate_got_vindicta_pops = yes
            event_target:gothones_remnant = {
                create_starting_leaders = yes
                country_event = { id = game_start.9 }
            }
		}
    }

	planet = {
        name = "Evol"
        class = pc_gas_giant
        orbit_distance = 100
        orbit_angle = 313
        has_ring = yes
		size = 38
